  "task_prompt": "## 任务：SCD Curriculum-Q (Fallback) Greedy Eval\n\n**目标**：Merge LoRA checkpoint → 跑 greedy inference on FewNERD test → 计算 micro-F1，与 baseline greedy F1 (0.748) 对比。\n\n**服务器**: autodl-5090-b\n**GPU**: cuda:2\n**exp_id**: `exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q`\n\n### 信息\n\n- LoRA checkpoint: `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-fallback-lora/`\n- Base model: Qwen3-8B（检查路径，可能在 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/` 下）\n- 训练参数: seed=43, 4706 steps, 2 epochs, train_loss=0.023, eval_loss=0.033\n- FewNERD test data: 应该在 `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/data/` 下\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. SSH 到 autodl-5090-b，检查环境和文件：\n   ```bash\n   ls /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-fallback-lora/\n   # 找 base model\n   find /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache -name \"config.json\" -path \"*Qwen*8B*\" 2>/dev/null | head -5\n   ```\n\n2. 检查同一台机器上 standard eval Worker 怎么跑的（参考它的方法）：\n   ```bash\n   # 查看是否有现成的 eval 脚本\n   find /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie -name \"*eval*\" -o -name \"*infer*\" -o -name \"*greedy*\" | head -10\n   ```\n\n3. **Merge LoRA**（如果需要的话，有些框架可以直接加载 adapter 推理）：\n   - 使用 LLaMA-Factory 的 export 命令，或者用 PEFT 的 merge_and_unload\n   - 如果 standard eval Worker 已经有了 merge 脚本，复用它\n\n4. **Greedy inference** on FewNERD test set：\n   - 使用项目的 `run_mvp_pilot.py` 或类似的推理脚本\n   - 参数：temperature=0（greedy），n_samples=1\n   - 输出预测结果\n\n5. **评估**：计算 micro-F1 (entity-level strict matching)\n\n6. **更新 registry**：\n   - 添加 metrics: greedy_f1, delta_vs_baseline\n   - 如果 greedy F1 > 0.748 (baseline): SCD 有效\n   - 如果 greedy F1 ≤ 0.748: SCD 无效\n\n**关键**：使用 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 确保只用 cuda:2。环境激活用 `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`。\n\n**注意**：autodl-5090-b 上同时有 standard eval 在 cuda:1 跑。不要冲突。\n",

    "snippet": "1\t# Handover — 2026-05-20\n2\t\n3\t> 由 Agent 生成，每次状态变更后自动更新。硬限 150 行。\n4\t\n5\t## 运行中\n6\t| exp_id | 假设 | 状态 | 关键指标 | W&B |\n7\t|--------|------|------|---------|-----|\n8\t| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\n9\t| exp_tlow_fewnerd_inference | Lower temperature (T=0.5) sampling on FewNERD produces less diverse but higher-quality samples that, when combined with T=1.0 samples in heterogeneous-temperature selection, improve self-consistency-based quality estimation compared to single-temperature baselines. | — | — | — |\n10\t| exp_b1_scd_threshold_ablation | Construction threshold sweep for SCD pseudo-label generation on FewNERD: θ∈{1/N, 2/N, 3/N, 0.5}. Higher θ = more conservative pseudo-labels (higher precision, lower recall). Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | — |\n11\t\n12\t## 待处理\n13\t- exp_spider_sc_pilot: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) validated on NER structured generation will also manifest in SQL structured generation with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on Spider, demonstrating cross-task universality of the diagnostic framework.（等待人工审批）\n14\t- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\n15\t- exp_a3_ace05_re: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade on ACE05 Relation Extraction. Closest extension to NER—same structured prediction paradigm, different output structure (entity pairs + relation types). Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct few-shot prompt. If correlation-selection gap exists for RE, the framework covers multiple IE sub-tasks.（排队等待 GPU）\n16\t- eval_scd_fewnerd_fallback（准备中）\n17\t- exp_b_frontier_sc_fewnerd: The correlation-selection gap in structured self-consistency persists with frontier models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.6), indicating it is a fundamental limitation of frequency-based SC for structured prediction rather than a capability issue of smaller models.（准备中）\n18\t- exp_a2_humaneval_sc: SC failure modes (degeneracy, false agreement) generalize from NER to code generation despite deterministic verification — majority voting on HumanEval will show limited gains over greedy when degeneracy is high, paralleling NER findings.（准备中）\n19\t- exp_a1_spider_sql_sc: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) applies to SQL structured generation on Spider. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct base model, 200 dev examples, N=8 T=1.0, execution accuracy evaluation. If F1-F4 failure modes exist in SQL, the framework generalizes beyond NER.（准备中）\n20\t- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\n21\t- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\n22\t- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\n23\t- …另有 9 项\n24\t\n25\t## 近期结论\n26\t- **exp_e_theta_adaptive** (negative): Threshold-Adaptive SC: N inverse scaling (D191) proves θ choice determines scaling direction. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline.\n27\t  [E_theta V2 MLP Results](/api/workspace-files/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/exp_e_theta_adaptive/data/v2_mlp_results.json)\n28\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q** (success): Q variant SCD with curriculum ordering: use B2 entity verifier confidence to stratify pseudo-labels (conf>0.8→Round1, 0.5-0.8→Round2, conf<0.5→discard). Curriculum by verifier confidence improves SCD convergence and final greedy F1 vs standard flat SCD on FewNERD.\n29\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard** (success): Standard Self-Consistency Distillation on FewNERD: fine-tune Qwen3-8B-FT with theta2n construction pseudo-labels (32,565 instances) mixed 1:1 with gold data. Greedy F1 after SCD should approach N=8 construction F1 (0.764 vs current 0.748). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.\n30\t\n31\t## 趋势\n32\t成功 107 / 负向 43 / 失败 4 / 共 184\n33\t\n34\t## 下一步\n35\t（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\n36\t\n37\t## 资源状态\n38\tautodl-5090: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n39\tautodl-5090-b: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n40\tautodl-5090-c: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n41\t\n42\t## 告警\n43\t- ft_002: 已被终止 — unknown\n44\t- mvp_pilot_002: 训练失败 — unknown\n45\t- exp_007_ner_llama: 已被终止 — unknown\n46\t- exp001_seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\n47\t- exp001_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n48\t- exp008_re_n16: 已被终止 — unknown\n49\t- exp_002_merge_conll2003: 已被终止 — unknown\n50\t- exp_001_n16_seed123_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n51\t- exp_001_n16_seed456_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n52\t- exp_008_re_n16_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n53\t- exp_001_seed42_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n54\t- exp_001_seed123_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n55\t- exp_001_seed456_v2_ner_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n56\t- exp_002_conll_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n57\t- exp_007_llama_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n58\t- exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n59\t- exp_019_supervised_verifier: 已被终止 — unknown\n60\t- exp_lp_t07_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\n61\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune: 已被终止 — unknown\n62\t- exp_freeform_seed789_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n63\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n64\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v3: 训练失败 — unknown\n65\t- exp_qwen3_4b_conll_merge_inference_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n66\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_merge_inference: 已被终止 — unknown\n67\t- exp_tacred_re_benchmark: 已被终止 — unknown\n68\t- exp_vc_lp_cascade_selection: 已被终止 — unknown\n69\t- hidden_state_probe_conll_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n70\t- hidden_state_probe_conll_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n71\t- dgs_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n72\t- mbr_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n73\t- exp_031_fewnerd_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n74\t- exp_030_conll_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n75\t- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: 已被终止 — unknown\n76\t- exp_14b_inference_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown"

  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：SCD Curriculum-Q (Fallback) Greedy Eval\n\n**目标**：Merge LoRA checkpoint → 跑 greedy inference on FewNERD test → 计算 micro-F1，与 baseline greedy F1 (0.748) 对比。\n\n**服务器**: autodl-5090-b\n**GPU**: cuda:2\n**exp_id**: `exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q`\n\n### 信息\n\n- LoRA checkpoint: `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-fallback-lora/`\n- Base model: Qwen3-8B（在 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/` 下搜索）\n- 训练参数: seed=43, 4706 steps, 2 epochs, train_loss=0.023, eval_loss=0.033\n- FewNERD test data: `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/data/` 下\n\n### 参考\n\n同一台机器上 standard eval Worker 已在 cuda:1 运行 (`worker_eval_exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard`)。查看它的方法作为参考：\n```bash\nps aux | grep -i \"eval\\|infer\\|lora\\|merge\" | grep -v grep | head -10\nls /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/eval_*.py 2>/dev/null\nls /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code/eval_*.py 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. SSH 到 autodl-5090-b，检查 checkpoint 和 base model：\n   ```bash\n   ls /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-fallback-lora/\n   find /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache -maxdepth 2 -name \"config.json\" -path \"*Qwen*8B*\" 2>/dev/null\n   ```\n\n2. 检查有没有已有的 eval/inference 脚本可复用：\n   ```bash\n   find /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie -name \"*eval*\" -o -name \"*greedy*\" -o -name \"*infer*\" 2>/dev/null | grep -v __pycache__ | head -20\n   ```\n\n3. **Merge LoRA + Greedy Inference**:\n   方案 A（推荐）: 使用项目的 `run_mvp_pilot.py` 加载 merged model 跑 greedy\n   方案 B: 使用 LLaMA-Factory export 合并，再用 vLLM 加载推理\n   方案 C: 使用 PEFT 直接加载 adapter 推理（不需要 merge）\n\n   关键：temperature=0（greedy），对 FewNERD 全部 test instances 跑一遍。\n\n4. **评估**：计算 entity-level micro-F1 (strict matching)。\n\n5. **更新 registry**（用 update_registry）：\n   - metrics 添加: eval_greedy_f1, delta_vs_baseline (baseline=0.748)\n   - 如果 greedy F1 > baseline: notes 写 \"SCD curriculum_q effective\"\n   - 如果 greedy F1 ≤ baseline: notes 写 \"SCD curriculum_q no improvement\"\n\n**关键**：使用 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2` 确保只用 cuda:2。环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`。\n\n**注意**：standard eval 在 cuda:1 同时跑，不要冲突。\n",

Higher θ = more conservative pseudo-labels (higher precision, lower recall). Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | — |\n11\t\n12\t## 待处理\n13\t- exp_spider_sc_pilot: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) validated on NER structured generation will also manifest in SQL structured generation with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on Spider, demonstrating cross-task universality of the diagnostic framework.（等待人工审批）\n14\t- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\n15\t- exp_a3_ace05_re: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade on ACE05 Relation Extraction. Closest extension to NER—same structured prediction paradigm, different output structure (entity pairs + relation types). Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct few-shot prompt. If correlation-selection gap exists for RE, the framework covers multiple IE sub-tasks.（排队等待 GPU）\n16\t- eval_scd_fewnerd_fallback（准备中）\n17\t- exp_b_frontier_sc_fewnerd: The correlation-selection gap in structured self-consistency persists with frontier models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.6), indicating it is a fundamental limitation of frequency-based SC for structured prediction rather than a capability issue of smaller models.（准备中）\n18\t- exp_a2_humaneval_sc: SC failure modes (degeneracy, false agreement) generalize from NER to code generation despite deterministic verification — majority voting on HumanEval will show limited gains over greedy when degeneracy is high, paralleling NER findings.（准备中）\n19\t- exp_a1_spider_sql_sc: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) applies to SQL structured generation on Spider. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct base model, 200 dev examples, N=8 T=1.0, execution accuracy evaluation. If F1-F4 failure modes exist in SQL, the framework generalizes beyond NER.（准备中）\n20\t- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\n21\t- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\n22\t- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\n23\t- …另有 9 项\n24\t\n25\t## 近期结论\n26\t- **exp_e_theta_adaptive** (negative): Threshold-Adaptive SC: N inverse scaling (D191) proves θ choice determines scaling direction. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline.\n27\t  [E_theta V2 MLP Results](/api/workspace-files/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/exp_e_theta_adaptive/data/v2_mlp_results.json)\n28\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q** (success): Q variant SCD with curriculum ordering: use B2 entity verifier confidence to stratify pseudo-labels (conf>0.8→Round1, 0.5-0.8→Round2, conf<0.5→discard). Curriculum by verifier confidence improves SCD convergence and final greedy F1 vs standard flat SCD on FewNERD.\n29\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard** (success): Standard Self-Consistency Distillation on FewNERD: fine-tune Qwen3-8B-FT with theta2n construction pseudo-labels (32,565 instances) mixed 1:1 with gold data.     "snippet": "1\t# Handover — 2026-05-20\n2\t\n3\t> 由 Agent 生成，每次状态变更后自动更新。硬限 150 行。\n4\t\n5\t## 运行中\n6\t| exp_id | 假设 | 状态 | 关键指标 | W&B |\n7\t|--------|------|------|---------|-----|\n8\t| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\n9\t| exp_tlow_fewnerd_inference | Lower temperature (T=0.5) sampling on FewNERD produces less diverse but higher-quality samples that, when combined with T=1.0 samples in heterogeneous-temperature selection, improve self-consistency-based quality estimation compared to single-temperature baselines. | — | — | — |\n10\t| exp_b1_scd_threshold_ablation | Construction threshold sweep for SCD pseudo-label generation on FewNERD: θ∈{1/N, 2/N, 3/N, 0.5}. Higher θ = more conservative pseudo-labels (higher precision, lower recall). Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | — |\n11\t\n12\t## 待处理\n13\t- exp_spider_sc_pilot: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) validated on NER structured generation will also manifest in SQL structured generation with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on Spider, demonstrating cross-task universality of the diagnostic framework.（等待人工审批）\n14\t- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\n15\t- exp_a3_ace05_re: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade on ACE05 Relation Extraction. Closest extension to NER—same structured prediction paradigm, different output structure (entity pairs + relation types). Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct few-shot prompt. If correlation-selection gap exists for RE, the framework covers multiple IE sub-tasks.（排队等待 GPU）\n16\t- eval_scd_fewnerd_fallback（准备中）\n17\t- exp_b_frontier_sc_fewnerd: The correlation-selection gap in structured self-consistency persists with frontier models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.6), indicating it is a fundamental limitation of frequency-based SC for structured prediction rather than a capability issue of smaller models.（准备中）\n18\t- exp_a2_humaneval_sc: SC failure modes (degeneracy, false agreement) generalize from NER to code generation despite deterministic verification — majority voting on HumanEval will show limited gains over greedy when degeneracy is high, paralleling NER findings.（准备中）\n19\t- exp_a1_spider_sql_sc: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) applies to SQL structured generation on Spider. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct base model, 200 dev examples, N=8 T=1.0, execution accuracy evaluation. If F1-F4 failure modes exist in SQL, the framework generalizes beyond NER.（准备中）\n20\t- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\n21\t- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\n22\t- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\n23\t- …另有 9 项\n24\t\n25\t## 近期结论\n26\t- **exp_e_theta_adaptive** (negative): Threshold-Adaptive SC: N inverse scaling (D191) proves θ choice determines scaling direction. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline.\n27\t  [E_theta V2 MLP Results](/api/workspace-files/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/exp_e_theta_adaptive/data/v2_mlp_results.json)\n28\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q** (success): Q variant SCD with curriculum ordering: use B2 entity verifier confidence to stratify pseudo-labels (conf>0.8→Round1, 0.5-0.8→Round2, conf<0.5→discard). Curriculum by verifier confidence improves SCD convergence and final greedy F1 vs standard flat SCD on FewNERD.\n29\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard** (success): Standard Self-Consistency Distillation on FewNERD: fine-tune Qwen3-8B-FT with theta2n construction pseudo-labels (32,565 instances) mixed 1:1 with gold data. Greedy F1 after SCD should approach N=8 construction F1 (0.764 vs current 0.748). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.\n30\t\n31\t## 趋势\n32\t成功 107 / 负向 43 / 失败 4 / 共 184\n33\t\n34\t## 下一步\n35\t（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\n36\t\n37\t## 资源状态\n38\tautodl-5090: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n39\tautodl-5090-b: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n40\tautodl-5090-c: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n41\t\n42\t## 告警\n43\t- ft_002: 已被终止 — unknown\n44\t- mvp_pilot_002: 训练失败 — unknown\n45\t- exp_007_ner_llama: 已被终止 — unknown\n46\t- exp001_seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\n47\t- exp001_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n48\t- exp008_re_n16: 已被终止 — unknown\n49\t- exp_002_merge_conll2003: 已被终止 — unknown\n50\t- exp_001_n16_seed123_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n51\t- exp_001_n16_seed456_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n52\t- exp_008_re_n16_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n53\t- exp_001_seed42_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n54\t- exp_001_seed123_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n55\t- exp_001_seed456_v2_ner_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n56\t- exp_002_conll_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n57\t- exp_007_llama_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n58\t- exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n59\t- exp_019_supervised_verifier: 已被终止 — unknown\n60\t- exp_lp_t07_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\n61\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune: 已被终止 — unknown\n62\t- exp_freeform_seed789_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n63\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n64\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v3: 训练失败 — unknown\n65\t- exp_qwen3_4b_conll_merge_inference_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n66\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_merge_inference: 已被终止 — unknown\n67\t- exp_tacred_re_benchmark: 已被终止 — unknown\n68\t- exp_vc_lp_cascade_selection: 已被终止 — unknown\n69\t- hidden_state_probe_conll_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n70\t- hidden_state_probe_conll_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n71\t- dgs_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n72\t- mbr_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n73\t- exp_031_fewnerd_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n74\t- exp_030_conll_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n75\t- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: 已被终止 — unknown\n76\t- exp_14b_inference_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown"

    "snippet": "1\t# Handover — 2026-05-20\n2\t\n3\t> 由 Agent 生成，每次状态变更后自动更新。硬限 150 行。\n4\t\n5\t## 运行中\n6\t| exp_id | 假设 | 状态 | 关键指标 | W&B |\n7\t|--------|------|------|---------|-----|\n8\t| exp_b_frontier_sc_fewnerd | The correlation-selection gap in structured self-consistency (where agreement correlates with accuracy but majority voting fails to improve over greedy) persists even with frontier models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.6), indicating it is a fundamental structural limitation of entity-level majority voting rather than a model capability issue. | — | — | — |\n9\t| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\n10\t| exp_tlow_fewnerd_inference | Lower temperature (T=0.5) sampling on FewNERD produces less diverse but higher-quality samples that, when combined with T=1.0 samples in heterogeneous-temperature selection, improve self-consistency-based quality estimation compared to single-temperature baselines. | — | — | — |\n11\t\n12\t## 待处理\n13\t- exp_spider_sc_pilot: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) validated on NER structured generation will also manifest in SQL structured generation with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on Spider, demonstrating cross-task universality of the diagnostic framework.（等待人工审批）\n14\t- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\n15\t- exp_a3_ace05_re: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade on ACE05 Relation Extraction. Closest extension to NER—same structured prediction paradigm, different output structure (entity pairs + relation types). Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct few-shot prompt. If correlation-selection gap exists for RE, the framework covers multiple IE sub-tasks.（排队等待 GPU）\n16\t- eval_scd_fewnerd_fallback（准备中）\n17\t- exp_a2_humaneval_sc: SC failure modes (degeneracy, false agreement) generalize from NER to code generation despite deterministic verification — majority voting on HumanEval will show limited gains over greedy when degeneracy is high, paralleling NER findings.（准备中）\n18\t- exp_a1_spider_sql_sc: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) applies to SQL structured generation on Spider. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct base model, 200 dev examples, N=8 T=1.0, execution accuracy evaluation. If F1-F4 failure modes exist in SQL, the framework generalizes beyond NER.（准备中）\n19\t- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\n20\t- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\n21\t- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\n22\t- exp_b2_v2_multi_domain_joint: Joint multi-domain training (CoNLL+SciERC+FewNERD LODO) improves entity verifier AUC and cross-domain construction F1 by exposing the model to diverse entity patterns. Tests whether training data diversity overcomes the calibration gap that caused B2 original to fail on FewNERD.（准备中）\n23\t- …另有 8 项\n24\t\n25\t## 近期结论\n26\t- **exp_e_theta_adaptive** (negative): Threshold-Adaptive SC: N inverse scaling (D191) proves θ choice determines scaling direction. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline.\n27\t  [E_theta V2 MLP Results](/api/workspace-files/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/exp_e_theta_adaptive/data/v2_mlp_results.json)\n28\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q** (success): Q variant SCD with curriculum ordering: use B2 entity verifier confidence to stratify pseudo-labels (conf>0.8→Round1, 0.5-0.8→Round2, conf<0.5→discard). Curriculum by verifier confidence improves SCD convergence and final greedy F1 vs standard flat SCD on FewNERD.\n29\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard** (success): Standard Self-Consistency Distillation on FewNERD: fine-tune Qwen3-8B-FT with theta2n construction pseudo-labels (32,565 instances) mixed 1:1 with gold data. Greedy F1 after SCD should approach N=8 construction F1 (0.764 vs current 0.748). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.\n30\t\n31\t## 趋势\n32\t成功 107 / 负向 43 / 失败 4 / 共 184\n33\t\n34\t## 下一步\n35\t（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\n36\t\n37\t## 资源状态\n38\tautodl-5090: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n39\tautodl-5090-b: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n40\tautodl-5090-c: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n41\t\n42\t## 告警\n43\t- ft_002: 已被终止 — unknown\n44\t- mvp_pilot_002: 训练失败 — unknown\n45\t- exp_007_ner_llama: 已被终止 — unknown\n46\t- exp001_seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\n47\t- exp001_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n48\t- exp008_re_n16: 已被终止 — unknown\n49\t- exp_002_merge_conll2003: 已被终止 — unknown\n50\t- exp_001_n16_seed123_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n51\t- exp_001_n16_seed456_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n52\t- exp_008_re_n16_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n53\t- exp_001_seed42_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n54\t- exp_001_seed123_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n55\t- exp_001_seed456_v2_ner_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n56\t- exp_002_conll_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n57\t- exp_007_llama_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n58\t- exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n59\t- exp_019_supervised_verifier: 已被终止 — unknown\n60\t- exp_lp_t07_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\n61\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune: 已被终止 — unknown\n62\t- exp_freeform_seed789_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n63\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n64\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v3: 训练失败 — unknown\n65\t- exp_qwen3_4b_conll_merge_inference_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n66\t- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_merge_inference: 已被终止 — unknown\n67\t- exp_tacred_re_benchmark: 已被终止 — unknown\n68\t- exp_vc_lp_cascade_selection: 已被终止 — unknown\n69\t- hidden_state_probe_conll_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n70\t- hidden_state_probe_conll_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n71\t- dgs_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n72\t- mbr_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n73\t- exp_031_fewnerd_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n74\t- exp_030_conll_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n75\t- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: 已被终止 — unknown\n76\t- exp_14b_inference_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown"

    "snippet": "1\t# Handover — 2026-05-20\n2\t\n3\t> 由 Agent 生成，每次状态变更后自动更新。硬限 150 行。\n4\t\n5\t## 运行中\n6\t| exp_id | 假设 | 状态 | 关键指标 | W&B |\n7\t|--------|------|------|---------|-----|\n8\t| exp_b_frontier_sc_fewnerd | The correlation-selection gap in structured self-consistency (where agreement correlates with accuracy but majority voting fails to improve over greedy) persists even with frontier models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.6), indicating it is a fundamental structural limitation of entity-level majority voting rather than a model capability issue. | — | — | — |\n9\t| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\n10\t| exp_tlow_fewnerd_inference | Lower temperature (T=0.5) sampling on FewNERD produces less diverse but higher-quality samples that, when combined with T=1.0 samples in heterogeneous-temperature selection, improve self-consistency-based quality estimation compared to single-temperature baselines. | — | — | — |\n11\t\n12\t## 待处理\n13\t- exp_spider_sc_pilot: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) validated on NER structured generation will also manifest in SQL structured generation with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on Spider, demonstrating cross-task universality of the diagnostic framework.（等待人工审批）\n14\t- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\n15\t- exp_a3_ace05_re: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade on ACE05 Relation Extraction. Closest extension to NER—same structured prediction paradigm, different output structure (entity pairs + relation types). Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct few-shot prompt. If correlation-selection gap exists for RE, the framework covers multiple IE sub-tasks.（排队等待 GPU）\n16\t- eval_scd_fewnerd_fallback（准备中）\n17\t- exp_a2_humaneval_sc: SC failure modes (degeneracy, false agreement) generalize from NER to code generation despite deterministic verification — majority voting on HumanEval will show limited gains over greedy when degeneracy is high, paralleling NER findings.（准备中）\n18\t- exp_a1_spider_sql_sc: Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) applies to SQL structured generation on Spider. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct base model, 200 dev examples, N=8 T=1.0, execution accuracy evaluation. If F1-F4 failure modes exist in SQL, the framework generalizes beyond NER.（准备中）\n19\t- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\n20\t- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\n21\t- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\n22\t- exp_b2_v2_multi_domain_joint: Joint multi-domain training (CoNLL+SciERC+FewNERD LODO) improves entity verifier AUC and cross-domain construction F1 by exposing the model to diverse entity patterns. Tests whether training data diversity overcomes the calibration gap that caused B2 original to fail on FewNERD.（准备中）\n23\t- …另有 8 项\n24\t\n25\t## 近期结论\n26\t- **exp_e_theta_adaptive** (negative): Threshold-Adaptive SC: N inverse scaling (D191) proves θ choice determines scaling direction. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline.\n27\t  [E_theta V2 MLP Results](/api/workspace-files/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/exp_e_theta_adaptive/data/v2_mlp_results.json)\n28\t- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q** (success): Q variant SCD with curriculum ordering: use B2 entity verifier confidence to stratify pseudo-labels (conf>0.8→Round1, 0.5-0.8→Round2, conf<0.5→discard). If F1-F4 failure modes exist in SQL, the framework generalizes beyond NER.（准备中）\n- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\n- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\n- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\n- exp_b2_v2_multi_domain_joint: Joint multi-domain training (CoNLL+SciERC+FewNERD LODO) improves entity verifier AUC and cross-domain construction F1 by exposing the model to diverse entity patterns. Tests whether training data diversity overcomes the calibration gap that caused B2 original to fail on FewNERD.（准备中）\n- …另有 8 项\n\n## 近期结论\n- **exp_e_theta_adaptive** (negative): Threshold-Adaptive SC: N inverse scaling (D191) proves θ choice determines scaling direction. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline.\n  [E_theta V2 MLP Results](/api/workspace-files/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/exp_e_theta_adaptive/data/v2_mlp_results.json)\n- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q** (success): Q variant SCD with curriculum ordering: use B2 entity verifier confidence to stratify pseudo-labels (conf>0.8→Round1, 0.5-0.8→Round2, conf<0.5→discard). Curriculum by verifier confidence improves SCD convergence and final greedy F1 vs standard flat SCD on FewNERD.\n- **exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard** (success): Standard Self-Consistency Distillation on FewNERD: fine-tune Qwen3-8B-FT with theta2n construction pseudo-labels (32,565 instances) mixed 1:1 with gold data. Greedy F1 after SCD should approach N=8 construction F1 (0.764 vs current 0.748). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.\n\n## 趋势\n成功 107 / 负向 43 / 失败 4 / 共 184\n\n## 下一步\n（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\n\n## 资源状态\nautodl-5090: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\nautodl-5090-b: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\nautodl-5090-c: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\n\n## 告警\n- ft_002: 已被终止 — unknown\n- mvp_pilot_002: 训练失败 — unknown\n- exp_007_ner_llama: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp001_seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp001_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp008_re_n16: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_002_merge_conll2003: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_001_n16_seed123_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_001_n16_seed456_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_008_re_n16_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_001_seed42_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_001_seed123_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_001_seed456_v2_ner_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_002_conll_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_007_llama_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_019_supervised_verifier: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_lp_t07_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_freeform_seed789_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v3: 训练失败 — unknown\n- exp_qwen3_4b_conll_merge_inference_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_merge_inference: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_tacred_re_benchmark: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_vc_lp_cascade_selection: 已被终止 — unknown\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_full: 已被终止 — unknown\n- dgs_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n- mbr_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_031_fewnerd_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_030_conll_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: 已被终止 — unknown\n- exp_14b_inference_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\n",

{
  "parentUuid": "6fc05494-6106-4986-bfac-62cc052d0db0",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/humaneval_sc/run_humaneval_sc.py",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/humaneval_sc/run_humaneval_sc.py",
        "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"HumanEval Self-Consistency Pilot: SC failure modes on code generation.\n\nCross-task universality extension — code has deterministic verification\n(unit tests), unlike NER's soft matching. Diagnoses degeneracy, agreement\npatterns, and whether majority voting helps when ground truth is executable.\n\nUsage:\n    export OPENROUTER_API_KEY=[REDACTED]\n    python run_humaneval_sc.py [--n_samples 8] [--temperature 1.0] [--seed 42]\n                               [--output_dir ./results] [--test_api]\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport itertools\nimport json\nimport math\nimport os\nimport random\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nimport textwrap\nimport time\nimport traceback\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\nfrom openai import OpenAI\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Constants\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nAPI_BASE_URL = \"http://47.94.22.126/v1\"\nMODEL_ID = \"openai/gpt-5.5\"\nHUMANEVAL_DATASET = \"openai/openai_humaneval\"\nEXEC_TIMEOUT = 30  # seconds per test execution\nMAX_RETRIES = 5\nRETRY_BASE_DELAY = 2.0\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Data loading\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef load_humaneval() -> list[dict]:\n    ds = load_dataset(HUMANEVAL_DATASET, split=\"test\")\n    problems = []\n    for item in ds:\n        problems.append({\n            \"task_id\": item[\"task_id\"],\n            \"prompt\": item[\"prompt\"],\n            \"canonical_solution\": item[\"canonical_solution\"],\n            \"test\": item[\"test\"],\n            \"entry_point\": item[\"entry_point\"],\n        })\n    return problems\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Prompt construction\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\n    \"You are a Python coding assistant. Complete the given function. \"\n    \"Return ONLY the function body (the code that follows the signature \"\n    \"and docstring). Do not repeat the function signature or docstring. \"\n    \"Do not include any explanation or markdown formatting.\"\n)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(problem: dict) -> list[dict]:\n    return [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": (\n            \"Complete the following Python function:\\n\\n\"\n            f\"```python\\n{problem['prompt']}```\\n\\n\"\n            \"Return only the function body code.\"\n        )},\n    ]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# API sampling\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef create_client() -> OpenAI:\n    api_key = [REDACTED]\"OPENROUTER_API_KEY\")\n    if not api_key:\n        [REDACTED] RuntimeError(\"OPENROUTER_API_KEY environment variable not set\")\n    return OpenAI(base_url=API_BASE_URL, api_key=[REDACTED]\n\n\ndef api_sample(\n    client: OpenAI,\n    messages: list[dict],\n    n: int = 8,\n    temperature: float = 1.0,\n    max_tokens: int = 1024,\n) -> list[str]:\n    for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):\n        try:\n            response = client.chat.completions.create(\n                model=MODEL_ID,\n                messages=messages,\n                n=n,\n                temperature=temperature,\n                max_tokens=max_tokens,\n            )\n            return [choice.message.content for choice in response.choices]\n        except Exception as e:\n            if attempt == MAX_RETRIES - 1:\n                raise\n            delay = RETRY_BASE_DELAY * (2 ** attempt) + random.uniform(0, 1)\n            print(f\"  API error (attempt {attempt+1}/{MAX_RETRIES}): {e}\")\n            print(f\"  Retrying in {delay:.1f}s...\")\n            time.sleep(delay)\n    return []\n\n\ndef api_sample_greedy(\n    client: OpenAI,\n    messages: list[dict],\n    max_tokens: int = 1024,\n) -> str:\n    for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):\n        try:\n            response = client.chat.completions.create(\n                model=MODEL_ID,\n                messages=messages,\n                n=1,\n                temperature=1.0,\n                max_tokens=max_tokens,\n            )\n            return response.choices[0].message.content\n        except Exception as e:\n            if attempt == MAX_RETRIES - 1:\n                raise\n            delay = RETRY_BASE_DELAY * (2 ** attempt) + random.uniform(0, 1)\n            time.sleep(delay)\n    return \"\"\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Code extraction — strip markdown fences and reconstruct full function\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef extract_code(raw_completion: str, prompt: str) -> str:\n    code = raw_completion.strip()\n    if code.startswith(\"```\"):\n        lines = code.split(\"\\n\")\n        lines = lines[1:]  # drop ```python\n        if lines and lines[-1].strip() == \"```\":\n            lines = lines[:-1]\n        code = \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n    # If the completion repeats the signature, try to use it as-is\n    entry_sig = prompt.strip().split(\"\\n\")[0]  # e.g. \"def foo(...):\"\n    if entry_sig in code:\n        return code\n\n    # Otherwise, prepend the prompt (signature + docstring) to the body\n    return prompt + code\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Safe test execution\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef execute_test(solution_code: str, test_code: str, entry_point: str,\n                 timeout: int = EXEC_TIMEOUT) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Run HumanEval unit tests against a solution. Returns {passed, error}.\"\"\"\n    check_fn = f\"\\ncheck({entry_point})\\n\"\n    full_code = solution_code + \"\\n\" + test_code + check_fn\n\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".py\", delete=False) as f:\n        f.write(full_code)\n        tmp_path = f.name\n    try:\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, tmp_path],\n            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout,\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            return {\"passed\": True, \"error\": None}\n        err = result.stderr.strip().split(\"\\n\")[-1] if result.stderr else \"unknown\"\n        return {\"passed\": False, \"error\": err}\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        return {\"passed\": False, \"error\": \"timeout\"}\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\"passed\": False, \"error\": f\"{type(e).__name__}: {e}\"}\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(tmp_path)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Evaluation metrics\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef pass_at_k(n: int, c: int, k: int) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Unbiased estimator of pass@k (Chen et al., 2021).\"\"\"\n    if n - c < k:\n        return 1.0\n    return 1.0 - math.prod((n - c - i) / (n - i) for i in range(k))\n\n\ndef evaluate(results: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Compute greedy, MV, random, and pass@k metrics.\"\"\"\n    metrics: dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n    greedy_pass = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"greedy_passed\"])\n    metrics[\"greedy_pass_rate\"] = greedy_pass / len(results)\n\n    # pass@1 and pass@8 (unbiased estimator)\n    pass1_vals, pass8_vals = [], []\n    for r in results:\n        c = sum(1 for s in r[\"sample_results\"] if s[\"passed\"])\n        n = len(r[\"sample_results\"])\n        pass1_vals.append(pass_at_k(n, c, 1))\n        pass8_vals.append(pass_at_k(n, c, min(8, n)))\n    metrics[\"pass@1\"] = np.mean(pass1_vals)\n    metrics[\"pass@8\"] = np.mean(pass8_vals)\n\n    # Majority voting: pick most common code among ALL samples, then check if it passes\n    mv_correct = 0\n    for r in results:\n        all_codes = [s[\"code\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"]]\n        code_counts = Counter(all_codes)\n        majority_code = code_counts.most_common(1)[0][0]\n        majority_passed = any(\n            s[\"passed\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"] if s[\"code\"] == majority_code\n        )\n        if majority_passed:\n            mv_correct += 1\n    metrics[\"mv_pass_rate\"] = mv_correct / len(results) if results else 0\n\n    # Random selection: expected pass rate = mean(c/n) per problem\n    random_pass_rates = []\n    for r in results:\n        c = sum(1 for s in r[\"sample_results\"] if s[\"passed\"])\n        n = len(r[\"sample_results\"])\n        random_pass_rates.append(c / n if n > 0 else 0)\n    metrics[\"random_pass_rate\"] = np.mean(random_pass_rates)\n\n    # Oracle: at least one sample passes\n    oracle = sum(\n        1 for r in results\n        if any(s[\"passed\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"])\n    )\n    metrics[\"oracle_pass_rate\"] = oracle / len(results)\n\n    return metrics\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Diagnostics\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef diagnose(results: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    diag: dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n    # Degeneracy: all N solutions are textually identical\n    n_degenerate = 0\n    for r in results:\n        codes = [s[\"code\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"]]\n        if len(set(codes)) == 1:\n            n_degenerate += 1\n    diag[\"degeneracy_rate\"] = n_degenerate / len(results)\n    diag[\"n_degenerate\"] = n_degenerate\n\n    # Agreement: fraction where majority agrees, and whether majority is correct\n    majority_agrees = 0\n    majority_correct = 0\n    for r in results:\n        codes = [s[\"code\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"]]\n        code_counts = Counter(codes)\n        top_count = code_counts.most_common(1)[0][1]\n        if top_count > len(codes) / 2:\n            majority_agrees += 1\n            top_code = code_counts.most_common(1)[0][0]\n            # Check if this majority code passes tests\n            matching = [\n                s for s in r[\"sample_results\"] if s[\"code\"] == top_code\n            ]\n            if matching and matching[0][\"passed\"]:\n                majority_correct += 1\n    diag[\"majority_agreement_rate\"] = majority_agrees / len(results)\n    diag[\"majority_correct_rate\"] = majority_correct / len(results)\n\n    # Execution agreement: group by pass/fail pattern\n    all_pass = sum(\n        1 for r in results\n        if all(s[\"passed\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"])\n    )\n    all_fail = sum(\n        1 for r in results\n        if not any(s[\"passed\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"])\n    )\n    mixed = len(results) - all_pass - all_fail\n    diag[\"all_pass\"] = all_pass\n    diag[\"all_fail\"] = all_fail\n    diag[\"mixed\"] = mixed\n\n    # Unique solutions per problem (diversity)\n    unique_counts = [\n        len(set(s[\"code\"] for s in r[\"sample_results\"])) for r in results\n    ]\n    diag[\"mean_unique_solutions\"] = np.mean(unique_counts)\n    diag[\"median_unique_solutions\"] = np.median(unique_counts)\n\n    # MV accuracy conditional on agreement\n    diag[\"mv_correct_given_agreement\"] = (\n        majority_correct / majority_agrees if majority_agrees > 0 else None\n    )\n\n    return diag\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# API connectivity test\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef test_api_connectivity(client: OpenAI) -> bool:\n    print(\"Testing API connectivity...\")\n    try:\n        resp = client.chat.completions.create(\n            model=MODEL_ID,\n            messages=[\n                {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"Return the number 42.\"}\n            ],\n            n=1,\n            temperature=1.0,\n            max_tokens=16,\n        )\n        content = resp.choices[0].message.content\n        print(f\"  API response: {content!r}\")\n        print(\"  API connectivity OK\")\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  API connectivity FAILED: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main pipeline\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef run_pipeline(args: argparse.Namespace):\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # --- Load data ---\n    print(f\"Loading HumanEval ({HUMANEVAL_DATASET})...\")\n    problems = load_humaneval()\n    print(f\"  Loaded {len(problems)} problems\")\n\n    # --- Client ---\n    client = create_client()\n\n    if args.test_api:\n        if not test_api_connectivity(client):\n            sys.exit(1)\n        print()\n\n    # --- Sample ---\n    results = []\n    checkpoint_path = output_dir / \"checkpoint.jsonl\"\n\n    # Resume from checkpoint if exists\n    completed_ids: set[str] = set()\n    if checkpoint_path.exists():\n        with open(checkpoint_path) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                rec = json.loads(line)\n                results.append(rec)\n                completed_ids.add(rec[\"task_id\"])\n        print(f\"Resumed {len(results)} completed problems from checkpoint\")\n\n    remaining = [p for p in problems if p[\"task_id\"] not in completed_ids]\n    print(f\"Sampling {len(remaining)} remaining problems \"\n          f\"(N={args.n_samples}, T={args.temperature})...\\n\")\n\n    for i, problem in enumerate(remaining):\n        task_id = problem[\"task_id\"]\n        print(f\"[{len(completed_ids)+i+1}/{len(problems)}] {task_id}\")\n\n        messages = build_prompt(problem)\n\n        # Stochastic samples\n        t0 = time.time()\n        raw_samples = api_sample(\n            client, messages,\n            n=args.n_samples,\n            temperature=args.temperature,\n        )\n        api_time = time.time() - t0\n        print(f\"  Sampled {len(raw_samples)} completions in {api_time:.1f}s\")\n\n        # Greedy sample\n        greedy_raw = api_sample_greedy(client, messages)\n\n        # Extract code\n        samples_code = [extract_code(s, problem[\"prompt\"]) for s in raw_samples]\n        greedy_code = extract_code(greedy_raw, problem[\"prompt\"])\n\n        # Execute tests\n        sample_results = []\n        for j, code in enumerate(samples_code):\n            test_result = execute_test(\n                code, problem[\"test\"], problem[\"entry_point\"]\n            )\n            sample_results.append({\n                \"idx\": j,\n                \"code\": code,\n                \"raw_completion\": raw_samples[j],\n                \"passed\": test_result[\"passed\"],\n                \"error\": test_result[\"error\"],\n            })\n\n        greedy_result = execute_test(\n            greedy_code, problem[\"test\"], problem[\"entry_point\"]\n        )\n\n        n_pass = sum(1 for s in sample_results if s[\"passed\"])\n        print(f\"  Tests: {n_pass}/{len(sample_results)} pass, \"\n              f\"greedy={'PASS' if greedy_result['passed'] else 'FAIL'}\")\n\n        record = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"prompt\": problem[\"prompt\"],\n            \"entry_point\": problem[\"entry_point\"],\n            \"greedy_code\": greedy_code,\n            \"greedy_passed\": greedy_result[\"passed\"],\n            \"greedy_error\": greedy_result[\"error\"],\n            \"sample_results\": sample_results,\n            \"n_samples\": len(sample_results),\n            \"n_pass\": n_pass,\n        }\n        results.append(record)\n\n        # Incremental checkpoint\n        with open(checkpoint_path, \"a\") as f:\n            f.write(json.dumps(record) + \"\\n\")\n\n    # --- Evaluate ---\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"EVALUATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    metrics = evaluate(results)\n    for k, v in metrics.items():\n        if isinstance(v, float):\n            print(f\"  {k}: {v:.4f}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {k}: {v}\")\n\n    # --- Diagnose ---\n    print(\"\\nDIAGNOSTICS\")\n    print(\"-\" * 40)\n    diag = diagnose(results)\n    for k, v in diag.items():\n        if isinstance(v, float):\n            print(f\"  {k}: {v:.4f}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {k}: {v}\")\n\n    # --- Save ---\n    final_output = {\n        \"config\": {\n            \"model\": MODEL_ID,\n            \"api_base\": API_BASE_URL,\n            \"n_samples\": args.n_samples,\n            \"temperature\": args.temperature,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n            \"n_problems\": len(problems),\n            \"timestamp\": time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"),\n        },\n        \"metrics\": metrics,\n        \"diagnostics\": diag,\n    }\n\n    with open(output_dir / \"summary.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(final_output, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    with open(output_dir / \"all_results.jsonl\", \"w\") as f:\n        for r in results:\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, default=str) + \"\\n\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_dir}/\")\n    print(f\"  summary.json  — metrics & diagnostics\")\n    print(f\"  all_results.jsonl — per-problem details\")\n    print(f\"  checkpoint.jsonl — incremental checkpoint\")\n\n    return final_output\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# CLI\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"HumanEval Self-Consistency Pilot\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_samples\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--temperature\", type=float, default=1.0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, default=\"./results\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--test_api\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"Test API connectivity before running\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    run_pipeline(args)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",
        "numLines": 512,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 512
      }
    },

NOT suitable for paper §5.\\\", \\\"n_inverse_scaling_validation\\\": {\\\"subset_n\\\": 4351, \\\"full_n\\\": 32565, \\\"subset_greedy_f1\\\": 0.7926, \\\"full_greedy_f1\\\": 0.7913, \\\"verdict\\\": \\\"Not a bug. Paper should note subset size.\\\"}}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\"}, \\\"exp_a2_degen_regression\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T10:13:37.615Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T10:13:55.891Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Degeneracy rate is primarily driven by task difficulty (dataset entropy ceiling) rather than model capability, with a positive correlation between base F1 and degeneracy — easy tasks with high F1 produce degenerate outputs because the model has already converged.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"n_points\\\": 30, \\\"linear_r2\\\": 0.414, \\\"quadratic_r2\\\": 0.592, \\\"spearman_rho\\\": 0.519, \\\"spearman_p\\\": 0.0033, \\\"linear_coeff\\\": \\\"degen = 129.5 × F1 - 56.9\\\", \\\"key_finding\\\": \\\"degeneracy driven by dataset difficulty (entropy ceiling), not model capability\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_e1_signal_routing\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T10:13:34.839Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T10:13:52.098Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"A config-level signal router (RF/LR) can predict the optimal construction signal per config from features (degeneracy_rate, base_f1, oracle_headroom), outperforming always-LP baseline. Router ΔF1 should exceed always-LP ΔF1.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"n_configs\\\": 16, \\\"rf_loo_accuracy\\\": 0.562, \\\"router_avg_delta_f1\\\": 0.39, \\\"always_lp_delta_f1\\\": 0.15, \\\"oracle_delta_f1\\\": 0.56, \\\"top_features\\\": [\\\"oracle_headroom\\\", \\\"degeneracy_rate\\\", \\\"base_f1\\\"], \\\"label_dist\\\": {\\\"lp_selection\\\": 6, \\\"none\\\": 5, \\\"consensus_theta2n\\\": 3, \\\"consensus_lp_weighted\\\": 1, \\\"majority_vote\\\": 1}}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"E1 signal routing. RF 56.2% LOO accuracy (below 60% target) but router ΔF1 +0.39pp > always-LP +0.15pp (2.6× improvement, target met). Low accuracy due to small n=16 and class imbalance. D176: appendix-only, not main claim.\\\"}, \\\"exp_pareto_diversity_quality\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T09:31:45.421Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T09:31:58.776Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"There exists a Pareto frontier in the diversity (1-degeneracy) vs construction quality (ΔF1) space across temperature configurations. Well-separated temperature mixing (T=0.7+T=1.0) achieves Pareto optimality for both uniform and VC construction, while VC-weighted construction flattens the trade-off slope, reducing the diversity cost of quality.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"dataset\\\": \\\"SciERC\\\", \\\"configs\\\": {\\\"t07\\\": {\\\"one_minus_degen\\\": 0.777, \\\"uniform_delta\\\": 1.81, \\\"vc_delta\\\": 1.17, \\\"pareto\\\": \\\"uniform only\\\"}, \\\"t08\\\": {\\\"one_minus_degen\\\": 0.819, \\\"uniform_delta\\\": 1.25, \\\"vc_delta\\\": 1.68, \\\"pareto\\\": \\\"VC only\\\"}, \\\"mix_07_10\\\": {\\\"one_minus_degen\\\": 0.83, \\\"uniform_delta\\\": 1.43, \\\"vc_delta\\\": 1.67, \\\"pareto\\\": \\\"both\\\"}, \\\"t10\\\": {\\\"one_minus_degen\\\": 0.877, \\\"uniform_delta\\\": 0.75, \\\"vc_delta\\\": 1.21, \\\"pareto\\\": \\\"both (highest diversity)\\\"}, \\\"mix_08_10\\\": {\\\"one_minus_degen\\\": 0.789, \\\"uniform_delta\\\": -0.17, \\\"vc_delta\\\": 0.47, \\\"pareto\\\": \\\"dominated\\\"}}, \\\"vc_slope_reduction\\\": \\\"~50% flatter than uniform\\\", \\\"key_insight\\\": \\\"well-separated T mixing dual Pareto optimal; close T mixing dominated\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"A4 Pareto diversity-quality analysis on SciERC. Mix T=0.7+T=1.0 is dual-Pareto optimal (uniform +1.43pp, VC +1.67pp). VC construction flattens trade-off slope ~50% vs uniform — increasing diversity costs less quality. Mix T=0.8+T=1.0 is dominated (close T → higher degeneracy, no construction gain). Confirms DGEC heterogeneous-T finding: ΔT matters, ΔT=0.3 beneficial, ΔT=0.2 harmful. Pareto plot artifact registered.\\\"}, \\\"exp_adaptive_sampling_budget\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T09:29:22.761Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T09:29:41.158Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Confidence-aware adaptive sampling budget allocation (fewer samples for high-confidence instances, full N=8 for low-confidence) can recover nearly all SC F1 gains while saving 40-50% of inference cost, because high-confidence instances already have low diversity and gain nothing from additional samples.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"model\\\": \\\"Qwen3-8B FT\\\", \\\"fewnerd\\\": {\\\"n_instances\\\": 37648, \\\"greedy_f1\\\": 0.7884, \\\"uniform_n8_f1\\\": 0.7819, \\\"best_adaptive\\\": \\\"h1_l8\\\", \\\"best_adaptive_f1\\\": 0.7849, \\\"best_delta_vs_u8\\\": \\\"+0.30pp\\\", \\\"sample_savings\\\": \\\"43.8%\\\", \\\"note\\\": \\\"Greedy > U8; adaptive h1/l8 best: greedy for high-conf, N=8 SC for low-conf\\\"}, \\\"scierc\\\": {\\\"n_instances\\\": 551, \\\"greedy_f1\\\": 0.6592, \\\"uniform_n8_f1\\\": 0.6712, \\\"best_adaptive\\\": \\\"top67%_h2_l8\\\", \\\"best_adaptive_f1\\\": 0.6707, \\\"best_delta_vs_u8\\\": \\\"-0.05pp\\\", \\\"sample_savings\\\": \\\"50.2%\\\", \\\"note\\\": \\\"U8 > Greedy; top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with negligible loss\\\"}}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"B7 adaptive sampling budget analysis on Qwen3-8B FT. Key findings: (1) FewNERD: greedy already beats uniform N=8 SC (+0.65pp), adaptive h1/l8 is optimal (+0.30pp vs U8, saves 43.8% samples); (2) SciERC: U8 beats greedy (+1.20pp), top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with only -0.05pp loss. Practical implication: confidence-gated sampling can halve inference cost. Artifacts: vc_8b_qwen3_ft_results, sj_8b_qwen3_ft_results (registered by worker).\\\"}, \\\"exp_dgec_sj_construction\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T08:37:58.013Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T09:26:30.567Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"SJ-weighted entity construction serves as control for VC — if SJ (soft entity overlap) works as well as VC (exact match consensus), any stronger QE signal suffices for construction; if VC specifically outperforms SJ, exact-match consensus has a unique advantage over soft similarity for entity-level selection.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"total_configs\\\": 26, \\\"significant_positive\\\": 1, \\\"significant_negative\\\": 15, \\\"not_significant\\\": 10, \\\"best_config\\\": \\\"scierc/72b/zs +2.0pp p=0.001\\\", \\\"worst_config\\\": \\\"fewnerd/8b_qwen3/ft mean -1.48pp all seeds p=1.0\\\", \\\"qwen3_8b_ft_supplement\\\": {\\\"configs\\\": 9, \\\"sig+\\\": 0, \\\"sig-\\\": 6, \\\"ns\\\": 3, \\\"fewnerd_mean_delta\\\": -1.48, \\\"scierc_mean_delta\\\": -0.4, \\\"conll_mean_delta\\\": -0.5, \\\"note\\\": \\\"SJ fully negative on Qwen3-8B FT — consistent with Qwen2.5-7B FT pattern\\\"}, \\\"m030_all_passed\\\": true}, \\\"notes\\\": \\\"COMPLETE (26 configs). SJ-weighted construction: 1 sig+ / 15 sig- / 10 ns. Qwen3-8B FT supplement (9 configs): 0+/6-/3ns — fully negative, consistent with 7B FT pattern. FewNERD -1.48pp mean (all seeds p=1.0). Definitively answers control question: NOT any stronger QE signal works for construction. SJ introduces noise at entity level. Only exception: SciERC 72B ZS (lowest precision, lowest degeneracy). Closed per D170/D171.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\"}, \\\"exp_dgec_vc_construction\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T08:37:55.968Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T09:26:29.776Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"VC-weighted entity construction outperforms greedy and LP-weighted construction across configs because Vote Count is a more direct quality estimator for structured outputs — it measures exact-match consensus without relying on model-internal log probabilities. Especially effective in high-degeneracy regimes where LP compression makes LP-weighting uninformative.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"total_configs\\\": 28, \\\"significant_positive\\\": 9, \\\"significant_negative\\\": 10, \\\"not_significant\\\": 9, \\\"by_dataset\\\": {\\\"fewnerd\\\": {\\\"configs\\\": 11, \\\"sig+\\\": 5, \\\"sig-\\\": 5, \\\"ns\\\": 1, \\\"mean_delta_pp\\\": -0.12}, \\\"wnut17\\\": {\\\"configs\\\": 3, \\\"sig+\\\": 2, \\\"sig-\\\": 0, \\\"ns\\\": 1, \\\"mean_delta_pp\\\": 0.92}, \\\"scierc\\\": {\\\"configs\\\": 7, \\\"sig+\\\": 2, \\\"sig-\\\": 0, \\\"ns\\\": 5, \\\"mean_delta_pp\\\": 0.62}, \\\"conll\\\": {\\\"configs\\\": 7, \\\"sig+\\\": 0, \\\"sig-\\\": 5, \\\"ns\\\": 2, \\\"mean_delta_pp\\\": -0.33}}, \\\"qwen3_8b_ft_supplement\\\": {\\\"configs\\\": 9, \\\"sig+\\\": 3, \\\"sig-\\\": 3, \\\"ns\\\": 3, \\\"best\\\": \\\"SciERC s42 +1.40pp p=0.017\\\", \\\"worst\\\": \\\"CoNLL s456 -0.54pp p=0.998\\\", \\\"note\\\": \\\"VC inconsistent on LP-success configs; seed-dependent on FewNERD; CoNLL all negative due to high degeneracy\\\"}, \\\"m030_all_passed\\\": true}, \\\"notes\\\": \\\"COMPLETE (28 configs). VC-weighted construction: 9 sig+ / 10 sig- / 9 ns. Qwen3-8B FT supplement (9 configs): 3+/3-/3ns — VC inconsistent even on LP-success configs. SciERC s42 best (+1.40pp) but cross-seed unstable. CoNLL all negative (high degeneracy). WNUT17/SciERC lean positive but not robust. Key answer: VC does NOT reliably improve where LP already works — seed-dependent, dataset-dependent. DGEC unified method goal (70-80% configs) unreachable at 32% (9/28). Closed per D170/D171.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\"}, \\\"exp_dgec_heterogeneous_t\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T08:37:59.963Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-20T08:49:58.009Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Mixing samples from T=0.7 and T=1.0 significantly reduces degeneracy compared to single-T sampling (by breaking temperature-induced mode collapse), enabling entity construction to achieve significant ΔF1 improvement even in configs where single-T construction was n.s. due to high degeneracy.\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"dataset\\\": \\\"SciERC\\\", \\\"n_instances\\\": 529, \\\"t07_degen\\\": 0.2231, \\\"t10_degen\\\": 0.1235, \\\"mixed_degen\\\": 0.1701, \\\"mixed_uniform_f1\\\": 0.6584, \\\"mixed_vc_f1\\\": 0.6609, \\\"cross_pool_uniform_delta\\\": \\\"+0.006 (1/3 seeds sig)\\\", \\\"cross_pool_vc_delta\\\": \\\"+0.004 (1/3 seeds sig)\\\", \\\"t08_t10_result\\\": \\\"harmful (degen increased)\\\", \\\"conclusion_preliminary\\\": \\\"partially_supported\\\"}, \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Hypothesis partially supported. T=0.7+T=1.0 mixed pool reduces degen (22.3%→17.0%) and improves construction (VC 3/3 seeds sig vs greedy), but cross-pool comparison (mixed vs T=1.0 alone) only 1/3 seeds significant. T gap matters: ΔT=0.2 (T=0.8+T=1.0) harmful, ΔT=0.3 (T=0.7+T=1.0) beneficial. VC weighting confirmed cross-T compatible. Awaiting Director conclusion classification.\\\"}}, \\\"count\\\": 15, \\\"total\\\": 15, \\\"baseline\\\": null}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.5904}}\"}",

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

(全集 96 种)\\n- **平均每文档**: ~11.5 个关系\\n- **文本长度**: 平均 1079 chars (最短 572, 最长 2873)\\n- **实体类型**: PER, ORG, LOC, TIME, NUM, MISC\\n\\n### 运行命令\\n\\n```bash\\ncd /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\n\\nCUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python3 experiments/docred_re/run_docred_re_sc.py \\\\\\n output/docred_re_sc\\n```\\n\\n### 预计时长\\n\\n- 200 instances × 9 passes (8 stochastic + 1 greedy) ≈ 1800 prompts\\n- DocRED 文档比 SciERC 长 (平均 1079 vs ~150 chars)，prompt 含 88 种关系类型的 schema hint (~1465 - ASB bugfix 报告 FewNERD pre-SCD greedy **micro**-F1 = 0.7911
- 如果 post-SCD 的 0.7866 也是 micro-F1，那 Δ = 0.7866 - 0.7911 = **-0.45pp**，SCD 实际变差了

**这会彻底翻转 SCD 从 +3.84pp 正向变为 -0.45pp 负向。**

**立即执行（不等其他任务）**：
1. 确认 SCD fallback eval 的 0.7866 是 micro-F1 还是 macro-F1？检查 `greedy_results.json` 中用的计算方式

   - Pre-SCD greedy micro-F1

   - Post-SCD greedy micro-F1
   - Post-SCD greedy macro-F1 (instance-average)
